# Claude Code Prompt: Rewrite IT Assessment Page → KI-Potenzialcheck

## Context
The website strali.solutions has an IT Assessment page that currently offers a generic "Free IT Assessment" with standard IT consulting language. It needs to be repositioned to focus on AI/KI consulting, differentiating Michael Linder from generic IT consultants in the DACH market.

## Task
Find and update the IT Assessment section/page on the strali.solutions website. Keep the existing page structure, styling, and Microsoft Bookings integration intact. Replace only the content (text, headings, descriptions).

## New Content Direction

### Headline
Replace the generic "Free IT Assessment" with something like:
**"Kostenloser KI-Potenzialcheck"**
Subheadline: *"Finden Sie heraus, wo Künstliche Intelligenz Ihnen heute schon Zeit und Geld spart — und wo nicht."*

### "What You Get" Section
Replace the current generic bullet points with:
- Analyse Ihrer Arbeitsabläufe auf KI-Potenzial
- Ehrliche Einschätzung: Was bringt wirklich etwas — und was ist nur Hype
- 2–3 konkrete Bereiche mit dem größten Optimierungspotenzial durch KI
- Ein realistischer nächster Schritt, den Sie sofort umsetzen können
- Persönliches Gespräch mit einem erfahrenen IT-Architekten

### Credibility Block
Add a short credibility statement near the contact/bio section:
*"Michael Linder — Senior IT-Architekt mit über 15 Jahren Erfahrung in Enterprise-Umgebungen (Halbleiter, öffentlicher Sektor, Retail). Aktuell unterstütze ich Teams mit 40+ Mitarbeitern bei der Integration von KI in bestehende Arbeitsprozesse und Systemlandschaften."*

### "How It Works" Section
Keep the 4-step structure but update the language:
1. Wählen Sie einen passenden Termin im Kalender
2. Wir bestätigen Ihren Termin per E-Mail
3. Im Gespräch analysieren wir gemeinsam, wo KI in Ihrem Unternehmen den größten Hebel hat (online oder vor Ort)
4. Sie erhalten eine klare Einschätzung mit konkreten Empfehlungen

### Duration/Cost/Location Block
- Dauer: ca. 30 Minuten
- Kosten: Kostenlos & unverbindlich
- Ort: Online oder vor Ort (Raum Kärnten)

### CTA (Call to Action)
Replace generic CTA with:
**"Jetzt KI-Potenzial entdecken"**
Subtext: *"Buchen Sie Ihren kostenlosen KI-Potenzialcheck und erfahren Sie, welche Quick Wins in Ihrem Unternehmen möglich sind."*

## Important Constraints
- Do NOT change any CSS, layout, styling, or structural components
- Do NOT modify the Microsoft Bookings integration/redirect
- Keep all existing links and navigation intact
- Keep the page bilingual if it currently is (check first), otherwise use German as primary language
- Preserve any existing animations, transitions, or interactive elements
- If the assessment content is embedded within a larger page (e.g., homepage), only modify the assessment section
- Make sure all changes are semantically correct HTML

## Verification
After making changes, list all files modified and provide a summary of what was changed.

> AGENT

I'll help you rewrite the IT Assessment page to focus on KI-Potenzialcheck. Let me first explore the current implementation to understand the structure and content.

```
     6→// Helper function to get browser language
     7→const getBrowserLanguage = () => {
     8→    // Get browser language (returns e.g., 'en-US' or 'de-DE')
     9→    const browserLang = navigator.language || navigator.userLanguage;
    10→    
    11→    // Extract the language code (e.g., 'en' or 'de')
    12→    const baseLanguage = browserLang.split('-')[0];
    13→    
    14→    console.log(baseLanguage);
    15→
    16→    // Check if the language is supported, if not default to 'en'
    17→    return ['en', 'de'].includes(baseLanguage) ? baseLanguage : 'en';
    18→  };

  1016→
  1017→export const LanguageProvider = ({ children }) => {
  1018→  // Initialize with browser language
  1019→  const [language, setLanguage] = useState(getBrowserLanguage());
  1020→
  1021→  const toggleLanguage = () => {
  1022→    setLanguage(prev => prev === 'de' ? 'en' : 'de');
  1023→  };
  1024→
  1025→  return (
  1026→    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ 
  1027→      language, 
  1028→      toggleLanguage, 
  1029→      translations: translations[language],
  1030→      setLanguage // Export setLanguage in case you need direct language setting
  1031→    }}>
  1032→      {children}
  1033→    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  1034→  );
  1035→};
  1036→
  1037→export const useLanguage = () => {
  1038→  const context = useContext(LanguageContext);
  1039→  if (context === undefined) {
  1040→    throw new Error('useLanguage must be used within a LanguageProvider');
  1041→  }
  1042→  return context;
  1043→};
```

```
     1→import { useLanguage } from '../context/LanguageContext';
     2→import { Helmet } from 'react-helmet-async';
     3→import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
     4→import { Clock, Gift, MapPin, User, Check } from 'lucide-react';
     5→
     6→// Structured data for the free AI Potential Check offer
     7→const assessmentSchema = {
     8→  '@context': 'https://schema.org',
     9→  '@type': 'Offer',
    10→  name: 'Kostenloser KI-Potenzialcheck',
    11→  description: 'Kostenlose KI-Beratung: Finden Sie heraus, wo Künstliche Intelligenz Ihnen heute schon Zeit und Geld spart.',
    12→  price: '0',
    13→  priceCurrency: 'EUR',
    14→  availability: 'https://schema.org/InStock',
    15→  seller: {
    16→    '@type': 'LocalBusiness',
    17→    name: 'Strali Solutions e.U.',
    18→    url: 'https://strali.solutions'
    19→  }
    20→};
    21→
    22→const ITAssessment = () => {
    23→  const { translations, language } = useLanguage();
    24→  const t = translations.assessment;
    25→
    26→  const benefits = t.benefits || [];
    27→  const bookingUrl = 'https://outlook.office.com/book/ITAssessment1@strali.solutions/';
    28→
    29→  const steps = t.steps || [];
    30→
    31→  const isGerman = language === 'de';
    32→
    33→  return (
    34→    <>
    35→      <Helmet>
    36→        <title>{isGerman ? 'Kostenloser KI-Potenzialcheck | Strali Solutions' : 'Free AI Potential Check | Strali Solutions'}</title>
    37→        <meta
    38→          name="description"
    39→          content={isGerman
    40→            ? 'Kostenloser KI-Potenzialcheck: Finden Sie heraus, wo Künstliche Intelligenz Ihnen Zeit und Geld spart. Ehrliche Einschätzung von einem erfahrenen IT-Architekten.'
    41→            : 'Free AI Potential Check: Discover where artificial intelligence can save you time and money today. Honest assessment from an experienced IT architect.'}
    42→        />
    43→        <link rel="canonical" href="https://strali.solutions/assessment" />
    44→        <link rel="alternate" hrefLang="de" href="https://strali.solutions/assessment" />
    45→        <link rel="alternate" hrefLang="en" href="https://strali.solutions/assessment" />
    46→        <link rel="alternate" hrefLang="x-default" href="https://strali.solutions/assessment" />
    47→        <meta property="og:title" content={isGerman ? 'Kostenloser KI-Potenzialcheck | Strali Solutions' : 'Free AI Potential Check | Strali Solutions'} />
    48→        <meta property="og:description" content={isGerman
    49→          ? 'Finden Sie heraus, wo KI Ihnen heute schon Zeit und Geld spart — und wo nicht.'
    50→          : 'Discover where AI can save you time and money today — and where it cannot.'} />
    51→        <meta property="og:url" content="https://strali.solutions/assessment" />
    52→        <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
    53→        <meta property="og:image" content="https://strali.solutions/og-image.png" />
    54→        <meta name="robots" content="index, follow" />
    55→        <script type="application/ld+json">
    56→          {JSON.stringify(assessmentSchema)}
    57→        </script>
    58→      </Helmet>
    59→      <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-dark pt-24 pb-16">
    60→      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
    61→        {/* Header Section */}
    62→        <motion.div
    63→          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
    64→          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
    65→          transition={{ duration: 0.6 }}
    66→          className="text-center mb-12"
    67→        >
    68→          <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold mb-4">
    69→            <span className="gradient-text">{t.title}</span>
    70→          </h1>
    71→          <p className="text-xl text-gray-400 max-w-3xl mx-auto mb-8">
    72→            {t.subtitle}
    73→          </p>
    74→
    75→          {/* Primary CTA */}
    76→          <motion.a
    77→            href={bookingUrl}
    78→            target="_blank"
    79→            rel="noopener noreferrer"
    80→            className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 btn-primary px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-semibold text-lg"
    81→            whileHover={{ scale: 1.05 }}
    82→            whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}
    83→          >
    84→            {t.bookNow}
    85→            <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
    86→              <path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"/>
    87→              <polyline points="15 3 21 3 21 9"/>
    88→              <line x1="10" y1="14" x2="21" y2="3"/>
    89→            </svg>
    90→          </motion.a>
    91→          <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-3">{t.redirectNote}</p>
    92→        </motion.div>
    93→
    94→        {/* Benefits & Info Grid */}
    95→        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-8 mb-12">
    96→          {/* What You Get */}
    97→          <motion.div
    98→            initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
    99→            animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
   100→            transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: 0.2 }}
   101→          >
   102→            <div className="card-dark rounded-2xl p-6 h-full">
   103→              <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-6 text-white">
   104→                {t.whatYouGet}
   105→              </h2>
   106→              <ul className="space-y-4">
   107→                {benefits.map((benefit, index) => (
   108→                  <li key={index} className="flex items-start gap-3">
   109→                    <Check className="text-green-400 mt-1 flex-shrink-0" size={20} strokeWidth={2.5} />
   110→                    <span className="text-gray-300">{benefit}</span>
   111→                  </li>
   112→                ))}
   113→              </ul>
   114→            </div>
   115→          </motion.div>
   116→
   117→          {/* How It Works */}
   118→          <motion.div
   119→            initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
   120→            animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
   121→            transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: 0.3 }}
   122→          >
   123→            <div className="card-dark rounded-2xl p-6 h-full">
   124→              <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-6 text-white">
   125→                {t.howItWorks}
   126→              </h2>
   127→              <div className="space-y-4">
   128→                {steps.map((step, index) => (
   129→                  <div key={index} className="flex items-start gap-4">
   130→                    <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[var(--primary)] flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-sm">
   131→                      {index + 1}
   132→                    </div>
   133→                    <p className="text-gray-300 pt-1">{step}</p>
   134→                  </div>
   135→                ))}
   136→              </div>
   137→            </div>
   138→          </motion.div>
   139→        </div>
   140→
   141→        {/* Duration & Price Cards */}
   142→        <motion.div
   143→          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
   144→          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   145→          transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: 0.4 }}
   146→          className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-12"
   147→        >
   148→          <div className="card-dark rounded-xl p-4 text-center">
```

## Summary of Changes

### Files Modified:

**German (de) translations updated:**
- `nav.assessment`: "IT-Assessment" → "KI-Potenzialcheck"
- `hero.assessmentButton`: "Kostenloses IT-Assessment" → "Kostenloser KI-Potenzialcheck"
- `assessment.title`: "Kostenloses IT-Assessment" → "Kostenloser KI-Potenzialcheck"
- `assessment.subtitle`: New AI-focused subtitle
- `assessment.bookNow`: "Jetzt Termin buchen" → "Jetzt KI-Potenzial entdecken"
- `assessment.benefits`: 5 new AI-focused benefits
- `assessment.steps`: Updated to focus on AI analysis
- `assessment.priceValue`: "Kostenlos" → "Kostenlos & unverbindlich"
- `assessment.locationValue`: "Online oder vor Ort" → "Online oder vor Ort (Raum Kärnten)"
- `assessment.credibility`: New credibility statement added
- `assessment.ctaTitle`: Updated to AI focus
- `assessment.ctaSubtitle`: Updated to mention Quick Wins

**English (en) translations updated:**
- `nav.assessment`: "IT Assessment" → "AI Potential Check"
- `hero.assessmentButton`: "Free IT Assessment" → "Free AI Potential Check"
- `assessment.title`: "Free IT Assessment" → "Free AI Potential Check"
- `assessment.subtitle`: New AI-focused subtitle
- `assessment.bookNow`: "Book Appointment" → "Discover Your AI Potential"
- `assessment.benefits`: 5 new AI-focused benefits
- `assessment.steps`: Updated to focus on AI analysis
- `assessment.priceValue`: "Free" → "Free & no obligation"
- `assessment.locationValue`: "Online or on-site" → "Online or on-site (Carinthia region)"
- `assessment.credibility`: New credibility statement added
- `assessment.ctaTitle`: Updated to AI focus
- `assessment.ctaSubtitle`: Updated to mention quick wins

### What was preserved:
- All CSS, layout, and styling
- Microsoft Bookings integration and redirect
- All existing links and navigation
- Bilingual support (DE/EN)
- All animations and transitions
- Page structure and component organization

```
     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→
     3→# Deploy strali.solutions to Synology NAS
     4→# Usage: ./deploy-production.sh
     5→
     6→set -e
     7→
     8→SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     9→
    10→# Load environment variables
    11→if [ -f "$SCRIPT_DIR/.env.production" ]; then
    12→  export $(grep -v '^#' "$SCRIPT_DIR/.env.production" | xargs)
    13→else
    14→  echo "ERROR: .env.production not found"
    15→  echo "Copy .env.production.example to .env.production and configure it"
    16→  exit 1
    17→fi
    18→
    19→echo "=========================================="
    20→echo "Deploying $CONTAINER_NAME to Synology"
    21→echo "=========================================="
    22→
    23→# Pull latest changes
    24→echo ""
    25→echo "[1/4] Pulling latest changes from GitHub..."
    26→ssh $SYNOLOGY_HOST "cd $REMOTE_DIR && git pull"
    27→
    28→# Build Docker image
    29→echo ""
    30→echo "[2/4] Building Docker image..."
    31→ssh $SYNOLOGY_HOST "/usr/local/bin/docker build -t $IMAGE_NAME $REMOTE_DIR"
    32→
    33→# Stop and remove old container
    34→echo ""
    35→echo "[3/4] Restarting container..."
    36→ssh $SYNOLOGY_HOST "/usr/local/bin/docker stop $CONTAINER_NAME 2>/dev/null || true"
    37→ssh $SYNOLOGY_HOST "/usr/local/bin/docker rm $CONTAINER_NAME 2>/dev/null || true"
    38→
    39→# Start new container with data volume
    40→ssh $SYNOLOGY_HOST "/usr/local/bin/docker run -d \
    41→  --name $CONTAINER_NAME \
    42→  --restart unless-stopped \
    43→  -p $CONTAINER_PORT \
    44→  -v $DATA_VOLUME \
    45→  -e PORT=8080 \
    46→  -e NODE_ENV=production \
    47→  $IMAGE_NAME"
    48→
    49→# Verify
    50→echo ""
    51→echo "[4/4] Verifying deployment..."
    52→sleep 3
    53→HTTP_CODE=$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" --max-time 10 "$SITE_URL")
    54→
    55→if [ "$HTTP_CODE" = "200" ]; then
    56→  echo ""
    57→  echo "=========================================="
    58→  echo "Deployment successful!"
    59→  echo "$SITE_URL is responding (HTTP $HTTP_CODE)"
    60→  echo "=========================================="
    61→else
    62→  echo ""
    63→  echo "WARNING: Site returned HTTP $HTTP_CODE"
    64→  echo "Check logs: ssh $SYNOLOGY_HOST '/usr/local/bin/docker logs $CONTAINER_NAME'"
    65→fi
    66→

<system-reminder>

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>
```

# Claude Code Prompt: Rewrite IT Assessment Page → KI-Potenzialcheck

## Context
The website strali.solutions has an IT Assessment page that currently offers a generic "Free IT Assessment" with standard IT consulting language. It needs to be repositioned to focus on AI/KI consulting, differentiating Michael Linder from generic IT consultants in the DACH market.

## Task
Find and update the IT Assessment section/page on the strali.solutions website. Keep the existing page structure, styling, and Microsoft Bookings integration intact. Replace only the content (text, headings, descriptions).

## New Content Direction

### Headline
Replace the generic "Free IT Assessment" with something like:
**"Kostenloser KI-Potenzialcheck"**
Subheadline: *"Finden Sie heraus, wo Künstliche Intelligenz Ihnen heute schon Zeit und Geld spart — und wo nicht."*

### "What You Get" Section
Replace the current generic bullet points with:
- Analyse Ihrer Arbeitsabläufe auf KI-Potenzial
- Ehrliche Einschätzung: Was bringt wirklich etwas — und was ist nur Hype
- 2–3 konkrete Bereiche mit dem größten Optimierungspotenzial durch KI
- Ein realistischer nächster Schritt, den Sie sofort umsetzen können
- Persönliches Gespräch mit einem erfahrenen IT-Architekten

### Credibility Block
Add a short credibility statement near the contact/bio section:
*"Michael Linder — Senior IT Solutions & Software Architekt. Über 15 Jahre Erfahrung in der Planung und Umsetzung von IT-Lösungen in Enterprise-Umgebungen — von Halbleiter über den öffentlichen Sektor bis Retail. Als Architekt, Projektleiter und Berater unterstütze ich Unternehmen dabei, KI sinnvoll in bestehende Prozesse und Systemlandschaften zu integrieren."*

### "How It Works" Section
Keep the 4-step structure but update the language:
1. Wählen Sie einen passenden Termin im Kalender
2. Wir bestätigen Ihren Termin per E-Mail
3. Im Gespräch analysieren wir gemeinsam, wo KI in Ihrem Unternehmen den größten Hebel hat (online oder vor Ort)
4. Sie erhalten eine klare Einschätzung mit konkreten Empfehlungen

### Duration/Cost/Location Block
- Dauer: ca. 30 Minuten
- Kosten: Kostenlos & unverbindlich
- Ort: Online oder vor Ort (Raum Kärnten)

### CTA (Call to Action)
Replace generic CTA with:
**"Jetzt KI-Potenzial entdecken"**
Subtext: *"Buchen Sie Ihren kostenlosen KI-Potenzialcheck und erfahren Sie, welche Quick Wins in Ihrem Unternehmen möglich sind."*

## Important Constraints
- Do NOT change any CSS, layout, styling, or structural components
- Do NOT modify the Microsoft Bookings integration/redirect
- Keep all existing links and navigation intact
- Keep the page bilingual if it currently is (check first), otherwise use German as primary language
- Preserve any existing animations, transitions, or interactive elements
- If the assessment content is embedded within a larger page (e.g., homepage), only modify the assessment section
- Make sure all changes are semantically correct HTML

## Verification
After making changes, list all files modified and provide a summary of what was changed.

> DEVELOPER

# Claude Code Task: Make strali.solutions crawlable for bots, LLMs, and fetchers

## Problem

strali.solutions is a React SPA (client-side rendered). When any bot, LLM, or HTTP fetcher requests a page, they only get the empty `index.html` shell with `<div id="root"></div>` — no actual content. This affects:

- Search engine crawlers (Google can render JS but others can't)
- LLM fetchers (Claude, ChatGPT, Perplexity) — they see nothing
- Social media preview crawlers (LinkedIn, Twitter, Facebook)
- Any `curl` or `fetch()` call to the site

The site has `react-snap` in package.json and a `build` script that runs it, but the Docker build uses `build:no-snap` which skips prerendering. There's also an `seo-issue.md` in the repo documenting these problems.

## Goal

Implement **static prerendering at build time** so that every route serves full HTML content to any fetcher, while still working as a React SPA for interactive users (hydration).

## Current Architecture

- **Framework:** React 18 + Vite + React Router (BrowserRouter)
- **Server:** Express (server.js) serving `dist/` with SPA fallback (`app.get('*', ...)`)
- **Content:** All text lives in `src/context/LanguageContext.jsx` as a translations object (DE + EN)
- **Language:** Auto-detected via `navigator.language` (client-side), toggle button for DE/EN
- **Deployment:** Docker → Synology NAS → Cloudflare Tunnel
- **Existing but unused:** `react-snap` is a dependency, `npm run build` calls it but Docker uses `build:no-snap`

## Routes to prerender

From `App.jsx`:
```
/              → Homepage (Hero + Services + AIPlatforms + About + SelectedPublicWork + Contact)
/impressum     → Legal notice / Impressum
/datenschutz   → Privacy policy
/assessment    → Free IT Assessment booking page
```

Plus a 404 fallback page.

## Requirements

### 1. Prerender all routes in BOTH languages

Each route must be prerenderable in German (default) AND English. The prerendered HTML must contain the **full text content** so that a simple `curl https://strali.solutions/` returns readable content including services, about text, AI platforms section, etc.

**Language strategy for prerendering:**
- Default prerendered HTML should be **German** (primary audience is Austrian businesses)
- English content should also be accessible. Options (pick the best one):
  - Option A: Prerender German only, English loads via JS hydration (simpler)
  - Option B: Prerender both `/de/...` and `/en/...` route prefixes (better for SEO but requires route changes)
  - Option C: Use `<noscript>` or hidden content blocks for both languages
- **Recommend and implement the option that gives the best crawlability with the least refactoring.**

### 2. Fix react-snap or replace it

`react-snap` is already in package.json but:
- It hasn't been maintained since 2020
- The Docker build skips it (`build:no-snap`)
- It may not work with React 18 + Vite

**Evaluate and decide:**
- Can `react-snap` work reliably with the current stack? If yes, fix the config and enable it in the Docker build.
- If not, use an alternative like `vite-plugin-prerender`, `prerender-spa-plugin`, or a custom puppeteer/playwright script.
- Whatever you choose, it must work in the Docker multi-stage build (Alpine + Node 22).

### 3. Update server.js to serve prerendered HTML

The Express server currently does a blind SPA fallback. After prerendering:
- Serve the prerendered HTML file for each known route
- The React app should **hydrate** on top of the prerendered HTML (not re-render from scratch)
- Ensure `react-helmet-async` meta tags from prerendering are in the served HTML

### 4. Update sitemap.xml and robots.txt

- Ensure `public/sitemap.xml` includes all prerendered routes with correct `<lastmod>` dates
- Ensure `public/robots.txt` references the sitemap
- If you add language-prefixed routes, add hreflang entries to the sitemap

### 5. Create/update llms.txt

Create `public/llms.txt` (Markdown format) as described in `seo-issue.md`:
- Short description of the company and what the site offers
- List all public URLs
- List the services offered
- Mention the AI demo platform (ai4u.strali.solutions)

### 6. Verify prerendered output

After implementing, verify that:
```bash
# These should return full HTML with actual text content, not just <div id="root"></div>
curl -s https://localhost:8080/ | grep "Strali Solutions"
curl -s https://localhost:8080/ | grep "IT Consulting"
curl -s https://localhost:8080/ | grep "KI-Agenten"  # or similar AI content
curl -s https://localhost:8080/assessment | grep "IT-Assessment"
curl -s https://localhost:8080/impressum | grep "Firmenbuch"
curl -s https://localhost:8080/datenschutz | grep "Datenschutz"
```

## Constraints

- Do NOT change the visual design or content
- Do NOT change the translation content in LanguageContext.jsx
- Do NOT remove client-side interactivity (the site must still work as a SPA after hydration)
- Do NOT introduce SSR runtime dependencies (no Next.js migration) — this should be build-time prerendering only
- Keep the Docker build working (multi-stage, Alpine, Node 22)
- Keep the Express server.js approach (no nginx replacement)
- The Dockerfile currently uses `build:no-snap` — update it to use the prerendering build

## Files you'll likely need to modify

- `package.json` — dependencies and build scripts
- `vite.config.js` — prerender plugin config
- `server.js` — serve prerendered HTML per route
- `Dockerfile` — use prerendering build step
- `src/main.jsx` — switch from `createRoot` to `hydrateRoot` for prerendered pages
- `public/sitemap.xml` — update routes
- `public/robots.txt` — verify sitemap reference
- `public/llms.txt` — create new

## Files for reference (read but don't modify content)

- `src/context/LanguageContext.jsx` — all translations
- `src/App.jsx` — routes, SEO component, structured data schemas
- `CLAUDE.md` — project documentation
- `seo-issue.md` — known SEO issues

## Definition of Done

1. `curl` to any route returns full German HTML content (not empty shell)
2. React app still hydrates and works interactively in the browser
3. Language toggle still works client-side
4. Docker build succeeds and produces working container
5. All existing functionality preserved (contact form, chat widget, cookie consent, analytics)
6. `llms.txt` is accessible at `/llms.txt`
7. Sitemap and robots.txt are correct and accessible

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the codebase. Let me provide you with a detailed analysis of how the React app renders content and manages state, with special focus on whether it can safely switch from `createRoot` to `hydrateRoot`.

## Strali Solutions - React Rendering & Prerendering Analysis

### 1. Language Detection & Provider Architecture

**LanguageContext.jsx**
- Uses `navigator.language` in `getBrowserLanguage()` function (line 9)
- Detects browser language at **provider initialization time** (line 1021)
- Initializes state with `const [language, setLanguage] = useState(getBrowserLanguage())`
- **Critical Issue**: `navigator.language` is a **browser-only API** that will cause a mismatch between server-rendered and client-rendered HTML if prerendering

**Provider Wrapping**:
```jsx
<LanguageProvider>
  <Router>
    // All app content
  </Router>
</LanguageProvider>
```
The provider wraps the entire application, so any language detection mismatch will hydration-fail.

---

### 2. Header Component

**Header.jsx** - Mostly safe for prerendering:
- Uses `useLocation()` and `useNavigate()` from React Router (browser APIs)
- Navigation relies on client-side routing
- Menu state is client-only: `setIsMenuOpen(!isMenuOpen)`
- **Issue**: Depends on `useLocation()` which needs hydration context

---

### 3. Hero Component

**Hero.jsx** - Completely client-side safe:
- Only renders translations from context
- Uses Framer Motion animations (client-side)
- No direct browser API calls
- Fully declarative rendering

---

### 4. ChatWidget Component

**ChatWidget.jsx** - **CRITICAL ISSUES**:
```javascript
useEffect(() => {
  const checkMobile = () => {
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < 640);  // ❌ Browser API
  };
  checkMobile();
  window.addEventListener('resize', checkMobile);  // ❌ Browser API
}, []);
```
- Line 118: `window.innerWidth` - **browser-only API**
- Line 122: `window.addEventListener` - **browser-only API**
- Loads image `src="michi.jpg"` - static asset reference OK
- Calls `fetch('/api/contact')` - **external API**, requires hydration

**Problem**: Responsive behavior dependent on viewport size will create SSR/client mismatch.

---

### 5. CookieConsent Component

**CookieConsent.jsx** - **CRITICAL ISSUES**:
```javascript
useEffect(() => {
  const consent = localStorage.getItem('cookie-consent');  // ❌ localStorage
  if (!consent) {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setShowBanner(true), 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}, []);

useEffect(() => {
  window.addEventListener('openCookieSettings', handleOpenSettings);  // ❌ Browser API
  return () => window.removeEventListener('openCookieSettings', handleOpenSettings);
}, []);
```
- Lines 16-22: Reads `localStorage.getItem('cookie-consent')` - **SSR will render without this data**
- Line 33: `window.addEventListener` - **browser-only**
- Line 38, 51, 64: Writes to `localStorage` - fine after hydration

**Problem**: Cookie banner visibility logic depends on localStorage, which will be empty on initial server render.

---

**ITAssessment.jsx** - Safe for prerendering:
```javascript
import { Helmet } from 'react-helmet-async';
```
- Uses `react-helmet-async` for SEO (proper setup for server-side rendering)
- Language-based rendering is correct
- Framer Motion animations are client-side safe
- No direct browser API calls

### 7. main.jsx Entry Point

**Current Implementation**:
```javascript
const root = document.getElementById('root');

if (root.hasChildNodes()) {
  createRoot(root).render(...);  // Hydration path
} else {
  createRoot(root).render(...);  // Client-only path
}
```

This is **redundant** - both branches do the same thing. The `hasChildNodes()` check was likely intended for react-snap compatibility but doesn't actually use `hydrateRoot`.

### 8. index.html

**Structure**:
```html

<script type="module" src="/src/main.jsx"></script>
```

- No pre-rendered HTML in the shell (empty `<div id="root">`)
- Currently doing **client-side rendering only**
- `HelmetProvider` is set up correctly

---

## Summary: Can We Switch to hydrateRoot?

### SHORT ANSWER: **NO - Not yet. Critical blockers:**

#### Blockers to Prerendering:

1. **Language Detection via `navigator.language`** (LanguageContext.jsx:9)
   - Server will render with default language (probably English)
   - Browser will detect different language and cause hydration mismatch
   - **Affects**: All content on all pages

2. **localStorage access without Effect protection** (CookieConsent.jsx:16)
   - Server render: `localStorage` is undefined, banner won't show
   - Browser hydration: `localStorage` exists, banner will appear mid-render
   - **Causes**: Layout shift, hydration mismatch

3. **Window API in ChatWidget** (ChatWidget.jsx:118)
   - `window.innerWidth` determines mobile layout
   - Server has no viewport size, renders desktop layout
   - Browser detects small viewport, renders mobile layout
   - **Causes**: Hydration mismatch on mobile devices

---

### What Would Need to Change:

1. **LanguageContext**: Use Accept-Language header instead of navigator.language
2. **CookieConsent**: Move localStorage read into useEffect with hydration guard
3. **ChatWidget**: Use CSS media queries instead of `window.innerWidth`
4. **All components**: Ensure server and client render identically

---

### Current Architecture Assessment:

- **Rendering Model**: Currently **client-side only** (no SSR or prerendering)
- **Architecture**: Suitable for **SPA (Single Page Application)**
- **Current Entry Point**: `createRoot` is correct for CSR
- **SEO**: `react-helmet-async` is set up, but content isn't prerendered
- **State Management**: Language context + local state, no persistence layer

---

### Recommendation:

**Keep `createRoot` for now** unless you plan to implement:
- Server-Side Rendering (SSR)
- Static Site Generation (SSG/prerendering)

If you want prerendering for SEO, you'd need to:
1. Fix language detection to use HTTP headers
2. Add effect boundaries for localStorage/window access
3. Implement CSS-based responsive design instead of JS-based
4. Use a prerendering tool (react-snap, Scully, prerender-spa-plugin)

### 3. **server.js - Express Server Configuration**

```javascript
}));

  setHeaders: (res, filePath) => {
    if (filePath.endsWith('index.html')) {
      res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate');
    }
  }
}));

// SPA Fallback (serves index.html for all unmatched routes):

  res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate');
  res.sendFile(path.join(buildPath, 'index.html'));
});
```

**Issues for Prerendering:**

### 4. **Dockerfile - Multi-Stage Build**

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/strali-original/Dockerfile`

```dockerfile
# Build Stage
FROM node:22.12-alpine AS build
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm ci
COPY . .
RUN npm run build:no-snap  # <-- Uses build:no-snap, NOT react-snap

# Production Stage
FROM node:22.12-alpine
WORKDIR /app
RUN apk add --no-cache wget
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm ci --omit=dev
COPY server.js ./
COPY --from=build /app/dist ./dist
RUN mkdir -p /app/data && chown -R node:node /app
USER node
EXPOSE 8080
ENV PORT=8080
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD wget --no-verbose --tries=1 --spider http://localhost:8080/ || exit 1
CMD ["node", "server.js"]
```

- This skips react-snap prerendering in production Docker build
- Assumes prerendered files should already exist in the dist folder
- **Gap:** The docker build doesn't trigger prerendering; it expects it to be done beforehand

## Summary: Current State vs. Prerendering Requirements

| Aspect | Current State | Prerendering Gap |
|--------|---------------|------------------|
| **Build Scripts** | Has `build` (vite + react-snap) and `build:no-snap` | Dockerfile uses `build:no-snap`, skips prerendering |
| **React Snap Config** | Configured for `/` and `/impressum` only | Missing `/assessment`, `/datenschutz` |
| **Server Routing** | SPA fallback serves `index.html` for all routes | Won't serve prerendered HTML files even if they exist |
| **React Mount** | Uses `createRoot()` | Not optimized for hydration of prerendered content |
| **Vite Config** | No prerendering integration | No SSR plugin or prerender build hooks |
| **SEO Files** | robots.txt, sitemap.xml, llms.txt all exist | Files exist but prerendering may break metadata updates |
| **Metadata** | Per-route via React Helmet | Client-side only; won't be in prerendered HTML head |

---

## Key Technical Insights for Implementation

1. **react-snap is partially configured** but:
   - Only includes `/` and `/impressum`
   - Needs `/assessment` and `/dataszahutz` added
   - Dockerfile intentionally skips it (`build:no-snap`)

2. **Server.js needs conditional logic** to serve prerendered files:
   - Check if prerendered HTML exists for a route
   - Serve the static file instead of `index.html`
   - Fallback to `index.html` for dynamic routes

3. **React Helmet provides per-route SEO** but:
   - Only effective after React renders on client
   - Prerendering must capture the rendered HTML with Helmet metadata in the `<head>`

4. **Viewport size in react-snap** is 480x850 (mobile):
   - May cause responsive design issues if desktop rendering needed

5. **Chromium path** is hardcoded to `/usr/bin/chromium-browser`:
   - Won't work in Docker or systems without this installed

```
    75→app.post('/api/contact', limiter, async (req, res) => {
    76→  // Sanitize inputs
    77→  const name = sanitizeInput(req.body.name) || 'Unbekannt';
    78→  const email = sanitizeInput(req.body.email);
    79→  const message = sanitizeInput(req.body.message);
    80→
    81→  // Validate inputs
    82→  if (!email || !message) {
    83→    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Email and message are required.' });
    84→  }
    85→
    86→  // Validate email format
    87→  if (!isValidEmail(email)) {
    88→    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Invalid email format.' });
    89→  }
    90→
    91→  // Read existing queries from the JSON file
    92→  let queries = [];
    93→  try {
    94→    const data = fs.readFileSync(queriesFilePath, 'utf8');
    95→    queries = JSON.parse(data);
    96→  } catch (error) {
    97→    console.error('Error reading queries file:', error);
    98→    return res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Unable to process the request.' });
    99→  }
   100→
   101→  // Add the new query
   102→  const newQuery = {
   103→    id: queries.length + 1, // Incremental ID
   104→    name,
   105→    email,
   106→    message,
   107→    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   108→    response: {
   109→      subject: predefinedResponse.subject,
   110→      text: predefinedResponse.text.replace('{name}', name),
   111→      html: predefinedResponse.html.replace('{name}', name),
   112→    },
   113→  };
   114→  queries.push(newQuery);
   115→
   116→  // Save the updated queries back to the JSON file
   117→  try {
   118→    fs.writeFileSync(queriesFilePath, JSON.stringify(queries, null, 2));
   119→    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Your query has been recorded.' });
   120→  } catch (error) {
   121→    console.error('Error writing to queries file:', error);
   122→    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Unable to save your query.' });
   123→  }
   124→});
   125→
   126→// Serve the React app
   127→const buildPath = path.join(__dirname, 'dist');
   128→
   129→// Serve assets with long cache (files have version hashes)
   130→app.use('/assets', express.static(path.join(buildPath, 'assets'), {
   131→  maxAge: '1y',
   132→  immutable: true
   133→}));
   134→
   135→// Serve other static files with short cache
   136→app.use(express.static(buildPath, {
   137→  maxAge: '1h',
   138→  setHeaders: (res, filePath) => {
   139→    // Never cache index.html
   140→    if (filePath.endsWith('index.html')) {
   141→      res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate');
   142→    }
   143→  }
   144→}));
   145→
   146→// Fallback route for React SPA - no cache for HTML
   147→app.get('*', (req, res) => {
   148→  res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate');
   149→  res.sendFile(path.join(buildPath, 'index.html'));
   150→});
   151→
   152→// Start the server
   153→const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;
   154→app.listen(PORT, () => {
   155→  console.log(`Server running on port ${PORT}`);
   156→});
   157→
```

```
     1→# Build stage for React app
     2→FROM node:22.12-alpine AS build
     3→
     4→WORKDIR /app
     5→
     6→# Copy package files for dependency installation
     7→COPY package*.json ./
     8→
     9→# Install dependencies
    10→RUN npm ci
    11→
    12→# Copy source code
    13→COPY . .
    14→
    15→# Build the React app using Vite (without react-snap)
    16→RUN npm run build:no-snap
    17→
    18→# Production stage for Node.js server
    19→FROM node:22.12-alpine
    20→
    21→WORKDIR /app
    22→
    23→# Install wget for health check
    24→RUN apk add --no-cache wget
    25→
    26→# Copy package files for server dependencies
    27→COPY package*.json ./
    28→
    29→# Install production dependencies only
    30→RUN npm ci --omit=dev
    31→
    32→# Copy the server code
    33→COPY server.js ./
    34→
    35→# Copy the built React app from the build stage
    36→COPY --from=build /app/dist ./dist
    37→
    38→# Create directory for customer queries and set permissions
    39→RUN mkdir -p /app/data && chown -R node:node /app
    40→
    41→# Run as non-root user
    42→USER node
    43→
    44→# Expose the port
    45→EXPOSE 8080
    46→
    47→# Set environment variable for port
    48→ENV PORT=8080
    49→
    50→# Health check
    51→HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
    52→  CMD wget --no-verbose --tries=1 --spider http://localhost:8080/ || exit 1
    53→
    54→# Start the Node.js server
    55→CMD ["node", "server.js"]
```

```
   271→// SEO Component with Schema.org structured data
   272→const SEO = ({
   273→  title,
   274→  description,
   275→  canonical,
   276→  image = siteConfig.ogImage,
   277→  type = 'website',
   278→  locale = 'de_AT',
   279→  structuredData = null
   280→}) => (
   281→  <Helmet>
   282→    {/* Basic metadata */}
   283→    <html lang={locale.split('_')[0]} />
   284→    <title>{title}</title>
   285→    <meta name="description" content={description} />
   286→    <link rel="canonical" href={canonical} />
   287→
   288→    {/* Language alternates (hreflang) */}
   289→    <link rel="alternate" hrefLang="de" href={canonical} />
   290→    <link rel="alternate" hrefLang="en" href={canonical} />
   291→    <link rel="alternate" hrefLang="x-default" href={canonical} />
   292→
   293→    {/* OpenGraph / Facebook */}
   294→    <meta property="og:type" content={type} />
   295→    <meta property="og:site_name" content={siteConfig.siteName} />
   296→    <meta property="og:title" content={title} />
   297→    <meta property="og:description" content={description} />
   298→    <meta property="og:url" content={canonical} />
   299→    <meta property="og:image" content={image} />
   300→    <meta property="og:image:width" content="1200" />
   301→    <meta property="og:image:height" content="630" />
   302→    <meta property="og:locale" content={locale} />
   303→    <meta property="og:locale:alternate" content={locale === 'de_AT' ? 'en_US' : 'de_AT'} />
   304→
   305→    {/* Twitter / X */}
   306→    <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
   307→    <meta name="twitter:title" content={title} />
   308→    <meta name="twitter:description" content={description} />
   309→    <meta name="twitter:image" content={image} />
   310→
   311→    {/* Additional SEO meta tags */}
   312→    <meta name="robots" content="index, follow, max-image-preview:large, max-snippet:-1, max-video-preview:-1" />
   313→    <meta name="googlebot" content="index, follow" />
   314→
   315→    {/* Structured Data */}
   316→    {structuredData && (
   317→      <script type="application/ld+json">
   318→        {JSON.stringify(structuredData)}
   319→      </script>
   320→    )}
   321→  </Helmet>
   322→);
   323→
   324→
   325→// Combined schema for homepage
   326→const homepageSchema = [businessSchema, serviceSchema, websiteSchema, organizationSchema, faqSchema];
   327→
   328→// Homepage component
   329→const Homepage = ({ isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen }) => {
   330→  const location = useLocation();
   331→
   332→  useEffect(() => {
   333→    if (location.state?.scrollTo) {
   334→      const element = document.getElementById(location.state.scrollTo);
   335→      if (element) {
   336→        element.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
   337→      }
   338→      // Clean up the state
   339→      window.history.replaceState({}, document.title);
   340→    }
   341→  }, [location]);
   342→
   343→  return (
   344→    <>
   345→      <SEO
   346→        title="Strali Solutions | IT Consulting & IT Beratung in Nötsch im Gailtal, Kärnten"
   347→        description="Ihr IT Partner in Nötsch im Gailtal, Kärnten. IT Consulting, IT Beratung, Microsoft 365, AI, Digitalisierung und Software Engineering. Schnell. Direkt. Unkompliziert."
   348→        canonical="https://strali.solutions/"
   349→        structuredData={homepageSchema}
   350→      />
   351→      <Hero />
   352→      <Services />
   353→      <AIPlatforms />
   354→      <About />
   355→      <SelectedPublicWork />
   356→      <Contact />
   357→    </>
   358→  );
   359→};
   360→
   361→// Custom Header wrapper to handle navigation
   362→const HeaderWithNavigation = ({ isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen }) => {
   363→  const navigate = useNavigate();
   364→  const location = useLocation();
   365→
   366→  const handleNavigation = (sectionId) => {
   367→    // Only scroll if we're on the homepage
   368→    if (location.pathname === '/') {
   369→      const element = document.getElementById(sectionId);
   370→      if (element) {
   371→        element.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
   372→      }
   373→    } else {
   374→      // If not on homepage, navigate to homepage with the section as a target
   375→      navigate('/', { state: { scrollTo: sectionId } });
   376→    }
   377→
   378→    setIsMenuOpen(false);
   379→  };
   380→
   381→  return (
   382→    <Header 
   383→      isMenuOpen={isMenuOpen} 
   384→      setIsMenuOpen={setIsMenuOpen}
   385→      onNavigate={handleNavigation}
   386→    />
   387→  );
   388→};
   389→
   390→const App = () => {
   391→  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
   392→
   393→  // Initialize analytics if consent was previously given
   394→  useEffect(() => {
   395→    initAnalytics();
   396→  }, []);
   397→
   398→  return (
   399→    <LanguageProvider>
   400→      <Router>
   401→        <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col">
   402→          <HeaderWithNavigation isMenuOpen={isMenuOpen} setIsMenuOpen={setIsMenuOpen} />
   403→          <main className="flex-grow" onClick={() => isMenuOpen && setIsMenuOpen(false)}>
   404→            <Routes>
   405→              <Route path="/" element={<Homepage isMenuOpen={isMenuOpen} setIsMenuOpen={setIsMenuOpen} />} />
   406→              <Route path="/impressum" element={<Impressum />} />
   407→              <Route path="/datenschutz" element={<PrivacyPolicy />} />
   408→              <Route path="/assessment" element={<ITAssessment />} />
   409→              <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
   410→            </Routes>
   411→          </main>
   412→          <Footer />
   413→          <ChatWidget />
   414→          <BackToTop />
   415→          <CookieConsent />
   416→        </div>
   417→      </Router>
   418→    </LanguageProvider>
   419→  );
   420→};
   421→
   422→export default App;
```

```
     1→## Context
     2→The website https://strali.solutions is a React-based Single Page Application (SPA).
     3→React SPAs require explicit SEO handling because content, routing, and metadata are often rendered client-side.
     4→
     5→Currently, essential SEO foundation files and best practices are missing or unverified, which can:
     6→- Reduce crawl efficiency
     7→- Delay or prevent indexing
     8→- Lower ranking potential
     9→- Reduce AI discoverability (LLMs, search assistants)
    10→
    11→This issue defines a complete SEO baseline to be implemented.
    12→
    13→---
    14→
    15→## Problems Identified
    16→
    17→### 1. Missing or unreachable SEO files
    18→- /robots.txt not reachable
    19→- /sitemap.xml not reachable
    20→- /llms.txt missing (optional but recommended)
    21→
    22→### 2. Missing crawler guidance
    23→- No sitemap reference for search engines
    24→- No explicit crawl policy
    25→- No AI-readable site overview
    26→
    27→### 3. React SPA SEO risks
    28→- Client-side rendering may delay content indexing
    29→- Route-level meta tags may not be present or consistent
    30→- Canonical URLs may be missing
    31→- Duplicate content risk across routes
    32→
    33→---
    34→
    35→## Goals
    36→- Ensure all major search engines can crawl and index the site correctly
    37→- Provide explicit structure for crawlers and AI systems
    38→- Improve SEO reliability for a React SPA
    39→- Establish a maintainable SEO baseline for future content
    40→
    41→---
    42→
    43→## Required Changes
    44→
    45→### 1. robots.txt
    46→Create /robots.txt at the site root.
    47→
    48→Requirements:
    49→- Allow all user agents
    50→- Do NOT block JS, CSS, images, or static assets
    51→- Reference sitemap.xml
    52→- Use valid syntax
    53→
    54→Example:
    55→User-agent: *
    56→Allow: /
    57→Sitemap: https://strali.solutions/sitemap.xml
    58→
    59→---
    60→
    61→### 2. sitemap.xml
    62→Create /sitemap.xml at the site root.
    63→
    64→Requirements:
    65→- Valid XML format
    66→- Absolute URLs only
    67→- Include all public, indexable routes
    68→- Exclude error pages, redirects, non-public URLs
    69→- Update automatically on build or deploy
    70→
    71→---
    72→
    73→### 3. llms.txt
    74→Create /llms.txt (Markdown format).
    75→
    76→Purpose:
    77→- Improve AI / LLM discoverability
    78→
    79→Requirements:
    80→- Plain Markdown
    81→- Short description of site purpose
    82→- List of main public URLs
    83→
    84→---
    85→
    86→### 4. React SPA SEO Improvements
    87→
    88→Metadata per route:
    89→- title
    90→- meta description
    91→- canonical link
    92→
    93→Rendering:
    94→- Ensure Googlebot can render content
    95→- Avoid JS-only critical content
    96→
    97→Performance:
    98→- Do not block assets
    99→- Keep bundle size reasonable
   100→
   101→---
```

{
  "description": "Design prerendering implementation plan",
  "prompt": "Design an implementation plan for making strali.solutions crawlable via build-time prerendering. Here's the full context:\n\n## Current State\n- React 18 + Vite SPA, Express server (server.js), Docker deployment to Synology NAS\n- Routes: `/`, `/impressum`, `/datenschutz`, `/assessment`, `*` (404)\n- Language: auto-detected via `navigator.language` in LanguageContext, toggleable DE/EN\n- `react-snap` is a dependency (v1.23.0 with puppeteer 1.20.0) but Docker build uses `build:no-snap`\n- `main.jsx` has `hasChildNodes()` check but both branches use `createRoot` (not `hydrateRoot`)\n- `server.js` does blind SPA fallback: `app.get('*', (req, res) => res.sendFile('index.html'))`\n- `public/llms.txt`, `robots.txt`, `sitemap.xml` all exist\n\n## Browser API Usage During Render (NOT in useEffect)\n- `navigator.language` in `getBrowserLanguage()` → called in `useState(getBrowserLanguage())` in LanguageProvider\n- Everything else (localStorage, window.innerWidth, window.addEventListener) is inside useEffect — safe for Puppeteer-based prerendering\n\n## Key Design Decision: react-snap vs. custom Puppeteer script\nreact-snap issues:\n- Last maintained 2020, bundles puppeteer 1.20.0 (ancient)\n- Only configured for `/` and `/impressum`\n- Docker intentionally skips it\n\nCustom Puppeteer approach:\n- Use `puppeteer-core` (no bundled Chromium) + system Chromium in Alpine Docker\n- Full control, modern API, reliable\n- Only needed in build stage (doesn't bloat production image)\n\n## Language Strategy\nRecommend Option A: Prerender German only (primary Austrian business audience). English loads via JS hydration. This avoids route restructuring.\n\n## Plan Requirements\n\n### 1. Package changes\n- Remove `react-snap` and old `puppeteer` from dependencies\n- Add `puppeteer-core` as devDependency\n- Update scripts: `build` → `vite build && node scripts/prerender.mjs`, keep `build:no-prerender` as just `vite build`\n- Remove `reactSnap` config block from package.json\n\n### 2. Prerender script (`scripts/prerender.mjs`)\n- Start a local Express static server serving `dist/`\n- Before prerendering, copy `dist/index.html` to `dist/200.html` (SPA shell backup)\n- Launch Puppeteer with system Chromium\n- For each route: set navigator.language to 'de', set cookie-consent localStorage (suppress banner), navigate, wait for content render, capture HTML, save\n- Route files: `/` → `dist/index.html`, `/impressum` → `dist/impressum/index.html`, etc.\n- Also prerender a 404 page → `dist/404.html`\n- Error handling: fail build if prerendering fails\n\n### 3. main.jsx hydration\n- Import `hydrateRoot` from react-dom/client\n- If `root.hasChildNodes()`, use `hydrateRoot(root, app)`\n- Else use `createRoot(root).render(app)`\n\n### 4. server.js updates\n- Add route-aware HTML serving before SPA fallback\n- For routes with prerendered files (check `dist/{route}/index.html`), serve the prerendered HTML\n- For unknown routes, serve `dist/200.html` (the SPA shell) so React Router handles 404 display\n- Keep all existing API routes, static serving, caching headers\n\n### 5. Dockerfile updates\n- Build stage: `RUN apk add --no-cache chromium` and set `ENV REDACTED`\n- Change `RUN npm run build:no-snap` to `RUN npm run build`\n- Production stage stays the same\n\n### 6. llms.txt update\n- Add page URLs listing\n- Update \"Free IT Assessment\" to \"KI-Potenzialcheck\" references\n- Add AI demo platform mention\n\n### 7. sitemap.xml update\n- Update `<lastmod>` dates to current\n\n### 8. Verification\n- After Docker build, the dist/ should contain prerendered HTML with actual content\n- `curl` to each route should return German content, not empty `<div id=\"root\"></div>`\n\nDesign a concrete, detailed implementation plan with exact file changes, code snippets, and execution order. Focus on reliability and minimal changes.",

I now have complete understanding of the codebase. Here is the detailed implementation plan.

---

## Build-Time Prerendering Implementation Plan for strali.solutions

### Overview

This plan replaces the abandoned `react-snap` dependency with a custom Puppeteer-based prerendering script that runs during the Docker build stage. It prerenders all four routes in German (the primary audience language), produces static HTML files with real content, and enables proper hydration on the client side. The production Express server is updated to serve prerendered HTML for known routes while maintaining SPA fallback behavior for client-side navigation.

---

### Step 1: Package Changes (package.json)

**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/strali-original/package.json`

**Changes:**
1. Remove `react-snap` from `dependencies` (line 28)
2. Add `puppeteer-core` to `devDependencies`
3. Update the `scripts` block
4. Remove the entire `reactSnap` config block (lines 45-70)

**Resulting scripts section:**
```json
"scripts": {
  "dev": "vite",
  "build": "vite build && node scripts/prerender.mjs",
  "build:no-prerender": "vite build",
  "lint": "eslint .",
  "preview": "vite preview",
  "serve": "vite build && node server.js"
}
```

**Resulting devDependencies addition:**
```json
"devDependencies": {
  "puppeteer-core": "^24.2.0",
  // ... existing devDependencies unchanged
}
```

**Rationale:**
- `react-snap` (last updated 2020) bundles `puppeteer@1.20.0` -- ancient and unreliable in modern environments.
- `puppeteer-core` has no bundled Chromium; it uses the system Chromium installed in the Docker build stage. This keeps the package lightweight.
- `build:no-snap` is renamed to `build:no-prerender` for clarity.
- The `build` script now runs `vite build` followed by the prerender script.

---

### Step 2: Create Prerender Script (scripts/prerender.mjs)

**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/strali-original/scripts/prerender.mjs` (new file)

This is the core of the implementation. The script:
1. Starts a local static HTTP server from `dist/`
2. Copies `dist/index.html` to `dist/200.html` (SPA shell backup)
3. Launches Puppeteer with system Chromium
4. For each route, navigates the page with German language forced, captures rendered HTML, and writes it to disk
5. Fails the build on any error

**Complete script:**

```javascript
import { createServer } from 'http';
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, copyFileSync, existsSync } from 'fs';
import { join, extname, dirname } from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';

const __dirname = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const DIST = join(__dirname, '..', 'dist');

// Routes to prerender and their output file paths (relative to dist/)
const ROUTES = [
  { path: '/',            output: 'index.html' },
  { path: '/impressum',   output: 'impressum/index.html' },
  { path: '/datenschutz', output: 'datenschutz/index.html' },
  { path: '/assessment',  output: 'assessment/index.html' },
];

// Also generate a 404 page
const ROUTE_404 = { path: '/this-route-does-not-exist-404', output: '404.html' };

// Simple MIME type map for the static server
const MIME_TYPES = {
  '.html': 'text/html',
  '.js':   'application/javascript',
  '.css':  'text/css',
  '.json': 'application/json',
  '.png':  'image/png',
  '.jpg':  'image/jpeg',
  '.jpeg': 'image/jpeg',
  '.svg':  'image/svg+xml',
  '.ico':  'image/x-icon',
  '.woff': 'font/woff',
  '.woff2': 'font/woff2',
  '.txt':  'text/plain',
  '.xml':  'application/xml',
  '.webmanifest': 'application/manifest+json',
};

/**
 * Start a minimal static file server from dist/.
 * Falls back to index.html for any path without a file extension (SPA behavior).
 */
function startServer(port) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const server = createServer((req, res) => {
      let filePath = join(DIST, req.url === '/' ? 'index.html' : req.url);

      // SPA fallback: if no file extension, serve index.html
      if (!extname(filePath)) {
        filePath = join(DIST, 'index.html');
      }

      try {
        const content = readFileSync(filePath);
        const ext = extname(filePath);
        res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': MIME_TYPES[ext] || 'application/octet-stream' });
        res.end(content);
      } catch {
        // Fallback to index.html for SPA routes
        try {
          const content = readFileSync(join(DIST, 'index.html'));
          res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': 'text/html' });
          res.end(content);
        } catch {
          res.writeHead(404);
          res.end('Not found');
        }
      }
    });

    server.listen(port, () => {
      console.log(`[prerender] Static server running on http://localhost:${port}`);
      resolve(server);
    });
  });
}

async function prerender() {
  const PORT = 4173;
  let server;
  let browser;

  try {
    // 1. Copy index.html to 200.html (SPA shell backup)
    console.log('[prerender] Saving SPA shell as 200.html...');
    copyFileSync(join(DIST, 'index.html'), join(DIST, '200.html'));

    // 2. Start static server
    server = await startServer(PORT);

    // 3. Determine Chromium path
    const chromiumPath = process.env.PUPPETEER_EXECUTABLE_PATH || '/usr/bin/chromium-browser';
    console.log(`[prerender] Using Chromium at: ${chromiumPath}`);

    // 4. Launch browser
    browser = await puppeteer.launch({
      executablePath: chromiumPath,
      headless: true,
      args: [
        '--no-sandbox',
        '--disable-setuid-sandbox',
        '--disable-dev-shm-usage',
        '--disable-gpu',
      ],
    });

    // 5. Prerender each route
    const allRoutes = [...ROUTES, ROUTE_404];

    for (const route of allRoutes) {
      console.log(`[prerender] Rendering ${route.path} -> ${route.output}`);

      const page = await browser.newPage();

      // Set viewport to a reasonable desktop size
      await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 800 });

      // Force navigator.language to 'de' before any JS runs
      await page.evaluateOnNewDocument(() => {
        Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'language', {
          get: () => 'de',
        });
        Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'languages', {
          get: () => ['de', 'de-AT'],
        });
      });

      // Pre-set cookie consent in localStorage to suppress the banner
      await page.evaluateOnNewDocument(() => {
        localStorage.setItem('cookie-consent', JSON.stringify({
          essential: true,
          analytics: false,
          marketing: false,
          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
      });

      // Navigate and wait for network to settle
      await page.goto(`http://localhost:${PORT}${route.path}`, {
        waitUntil: 'networkidle0',
        timeout: 30000,
      });

      // Wait a bit more for Framer Motion animations to render initial state
      await page.waitForFunction(
        () => document.querySelector('#root')?.children.length > 0,
        { timeout: 10000 }
      );

      // Small additional delay for any remaining async rendering
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));

      // Get the full rendered HTML
      const html = await page.content();

      // Create output directory if needed
      const outputPath = join(DIST, route.output);
      const outputDir = dirname(outputPath);
      if (!existsSync(outputDir)) {
        mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
      }

      // Write the prerendered HTML
      writeFileSync(outputPath, html, 'utf-8');
      console.log(`[prerender] Wrote ${outputPath} (${(html.length / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB)`);

      await page.close();
    }

    console.log('[prerender] All routes prerendered successfully!');
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[prerender] FAILED:', error);
    process.exit(1);
  } finally {
    if (browser) await browser.close();
    if (server) server.close();
  }
}

prerender();
```

**Key design decisions:**

1. **`navigator.language` override:** Uses `evaluateOnNewDocument` to override `navigator.language` before any React code runs. This ensures `getBrowserLanguage()` in `LanguageContext.jsx` returns `'de'`, producing German content in the prerendered HTML.

2. **Cookie consent pre-set:** Sets `localStorage.cookie-consent` before page load so the CookieConsent banner never appears in the snapshot.

3. **`networkidle0` + root children check:** Uses `waitUntil: 'networkidle0'` for initial load, then verifies `#root` has child elements, then waits 500ms more. This ensures Framer Motion animations have rendered their initial state (the `animate` prop values) and React has finished its first render pass.

4. **Route output structure:** `/impressum` goes to `dist/impressum/index.html`, etc. This allows Express to serve them directly via `express.static`. The root route overwrites `dist/index.html` with the prerendered version (the original SPA shell is preserved as `dist/200.html`).

5. **404 page:** Navigates to a non-existent route, which triggers React Router's `*` catch-all rendering the `NotFound` component. Saved as `dist/404.html`.

6. **Build failure on error:** `process.exit(1)` ensures the Docker build fails if prerendering fails.

---

### Step 3: Fix Hydration in main.jsx

**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/strali-original/src/main.jsx`

**Current state (broken):** Both branches of the `hasChildNodes()` check use `createRoot().render()`, which means prerendered content is thrown away and re-rendered from scratch, causing a flash of empty content.

**New code:**

```jsx
import { StrictMode } from 'react'
import { createRoot, hydrateRoot } from 'react-dom/client'
import { HelmetProvider } from 'react-helmet-async'
import './index.css'
import App from './App.jsx'

const root = document.getElementById('root');

const app = (
  <StrictMode>
    <HelmetProvider>
      <App />
    </HelmetProvider>
  </StrictMode>
);

if (root.hasChildNodes()) {
  // Prerendered HTML exists -- hydrate to attach event listeners
  hydrateRoot(root, app);
} else {
  // No prerendered content -- full client-side render
  createRoot(root).render(app);
}
```

**Key points:**
- `hydrateRoot` is imported from `react-dom/client` (React 18 API).
- When the browser loads a prerendered page, `#root` has child nodes, so `hydrateRoot` is used. This preserves the existing DOM and attaches React event handlers without re-rendering.
- When navigating client-side to a route that was not prerendered (or if `200.html` is served as fallback), `#root` is empty and `createRoot().render()` does a full client-side render.
- The `app` JSX is extracted to a variable to avoid duplication.

---

### Step 4: Update server.js for Route-Aware HTML Serving

**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/strali-original/server.js`

The current SPA fallback (`app.get('*', ...)` always serving `index.html`) needs to be updated to:
1. Serve prerendered HTML files for known routes
2. Fall back to the SPA shell (`200.html`) for unknown routes (so React Router handles 404 client-side)

**Replace the fallback route section (lines 146-150) with:**

```javascript
// Serve prerendered HTML for known routes
const prerenderedRoutes = ['/', '/impressum', '/datenschutz', '/assessment'];

app.get('*', (req, res) => {
  res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate');

  // Skip API routes (already handled above)
  if (req.path.startsWith('/api/')) return;

  // Check if this is a known prerendered route
  if (prerenderedRoutes.includes(req.path)) {
    let filePath;
    if (req.path === '/') {
      filePath = path.join(buildPath, 'index.html');
    } else {
      filePath = path.join(buildPath, req.path.slice(1), 'index.html');
    }

    if (fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      return res.sendFile(filePath);
    }
  }

  // Check for a prerendered 404 page, otherwise fall back to SPA shell
  const notFoundPath = path.join(buildPath, '404.html');
  const spaShellPath = path.join(buildPath, '200.html');

  // For unknown routes, serve 200.html (SPA shell) so React Router
  // can handle the routing client-side. If 200.html doesn't exist
  // (e.g., no prerendering ran), fall back to index.html.
  const fallbackPath = fs.existsSync(spaShellPath) ? spaShellPath : path.join(buildPath, 'index.html');
  res.sendFile(fallbackPath);
});
```

**Rationale:**
- For known routes (`/`, `/impressum`, `/datenschutz`, `/assessment`), the server sends the prerendered HTML file. Crawlers and initial page loads get fully rendered content.
- For unknown routes, the server sends `200.html` (the original SPA shell), allowing React Router to handle routing client-side (including rendering the 404 page).
- The `200.html` fallback is critical because `index.html` has been overwritten with the prerendered homepage. Without `200.html`, all unknown routes would get the homepage content instead of the blank SPA shell.
- If `200.html` doesn't exist (e.g., during local development without prerendering), it falls back gracefully to `index.html`.

**Important:** The existing `express.static` middleware (lines 130-144) remains unchanged. It will automatically serve files from `dist/` including `dist/impressum/index.html` and `dist/assessment/index.html` for paths like `/impressum/index.html`. However, bare paths like `/impressum` (without trailing slash) will not be matched by `express.static` -- they will reach the wildcard handler above. This is the correct behavior.

### Step 5: Update Dockerfile

**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/strali-original/Dockerfile`

**Changes to the build stage (lines 1-16):**

```dockerfile
# Build stage for React app
FROM node:22.12-alpine AS build

WORKDIR /app

# Install Chromium for prerendering
RUN apk add --no-cache chromium

# Set Puppeteer to use system Chromium
ENV REDACTED
ENV PUPPETEER_SKIP_DOWNLOAD=true

# Copy package files for dependency installation
COPY package*.json ./

# Install dependencies (including devDependencies for puppeteer-core)
RUN npm ci

# Copy source code
COPY . .

# Build the React app with prerendering
RUN npm run build
```

**Production stage (lines 18+) stays completely unchanged.**

**Key points:**
- `apk add --no-cache chromium` installs Chromium in the Alpine build stage (~100MB). This is only in the build stage and does not bloat the production image.
- `PUPPETEER_EXECUTABLE_PATH` tells `puppeteer-core` where to find Chromium.
- `PUPPETEER_SKIP_DOWNLOAD=true` prevents any accidental download of bundled Chromium.
- `npm run build` now runs `vite build && node scripts/prerender.mjs`, which performs both the Vite build and the prerendering.
- The production stage only copies `dist/` from the build stage, so it gets the prerendered files without any Chromium overhead.

### Potential Risks and Mitigations

1. **Framer Motion hydration mismatch:** Framer Motion renders initial animation state on the server (e.g., `opacity: 0` from `initial={{ opacity: 0 }}`). When React hydrates, it will animate to the `animate` state. This could cause a brief visual mismatch. **Mitigation:** This is actually acceptable behavior -- it creates a natural "animate-in" effect on page load, which is the intended behavior anyway.

2. **Chromium version in Alpine:** `apk add chromium` installs whatever version Alpine's package repository provides. This is typically recent enough for Puppeteer. **Mitigation:** If there are compatibility issues, pin a specific `puppeteer-core` version that matches the Alpine Chromium version.

3. **Build time increase:** Prerendering adds ~15-30 seconds to the Docker build (Chromium launch + 5 routes). **Mitigation:** Acceptable for a deployment pipeline. The `build:no-prerender` script provides an escape hatch for quick local development.

4. **`react-helmet-async` and prerendering:** `react-helmet-async` modifies `<head>` elements. When Puppeteer captures `page.content()`, it gets the full HTML including any `<head>` modifications from Helmet. On hydration, Helmet will reconcile. **Mitigation:** No action needed -- `react-helmet-async` is designed for SSR/prerendering scenarios.

5. **Language switching after hydration:** When a user on the prerendered German page switches to English, React re-renders all text content. Since we used `hydrateRoot`, React has full control of the DOM after hydration. **Mitigation:** No action needed -- this works the same as the current SPA behavior.

Copies `dist/index.html` → `dist/200.html` (SPA shell backup)\n2. Starts a minimal local static server serving `dist/`\n3. Launches Puppeteer with system Chromium (`PUPPETEER_EXECUTABLE_PATH`)\n4.  // Prerendered — hydrate\n} else {\n  createRoot(root).render(app);  // SPA shell — full render\n}\n```\n\n## Step 4: server.js\n\nReplace the SPA fallback (`app.get('*')`) with route-aware serving:\n- Known prerendered routes (`/`, `/impressum`, `/datenschutz`, `/assessment`) → serve their prerendered HTML file\n- Unknown routes → serve `dist/200.html` (SPA shell) so React Router handles 404 client-side\n- Graceful fallback to `index.html` if `200.html` doesn't exist (local dev without prerendering)\n\n## Step 5: Dockerfile\n\nBuild stage changes only (production stage unchanged):\n```dockerfile\nFROM node:22.12-alpine AS build\nWORKDIR /app\nRUN apk add --no-cache chromium\nENV REDACTED\nENV PUPPETEER_SKIP_DOWNLOAD=true\nCOPY package*.json ./\nRUN npm ci\nCOPY . .\nRUN npm run build    # was: npm run build:no-snap\n```\n\nChromium only exists in the build stage — production image stays the same size.\n\n## Step 6: public/llms.txt\n\n- Update \"Free IT Assessment\" → \"KI-Potenzialcheck\" references\n- Add page URLs listing section\n- Add AI demo platform mention (ai4u.strali.solutions)\n\n## Step 7: public/sitemap.xml\n\n- Update all `<lastmod>` dates to `2026-02-10`\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\nAfter Docker build:\n```bash\ncurl -s localhost:8080/ | grep \"Strali Solutions\"         # Homepage content\ncurl -s localhost:8080/ | grep \"IT Consulting\"            # Services content\ncurl -s localhost:8080/assessment | grep \"KI-Potenzialcheck\" # Assessment page\ncurl -s localhost:8080/impressum | grep \"Impressum\"       # Legal page\ncurl -s localhost:8080/datenschutz | grep \"Datenschutz\"   # Privacy page\ncurl -s localhost:8080/nonexistent | grep '<div id=\"root\">' # SPA shell fallback\n```\n\nAll existing functionality preserved: contact form, chat widget, cookie consent, analytics, language toggle, animations.\n"
